establishment of factories and stations and facilities for trade, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-44">6</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/07-chapter-iii-the-native-states/#page-109">71</a>, at Madras in 1639,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-44">6</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/07-chapter-iii-the-native-states/#page-109">71</a>, Bombay in 1668, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-44">6</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/07-chapter-iii-the-native-states/#page-109">71</a>, at<br>Hooghly in 1644, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-44">6</a>; foundation of<br>Calcutta by Job Charnock in 1686, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-44">6</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/07-chapter-iii-the-native-states/#page-109">71</a>; rivalry between ‘London’ and<br>‘English’ Companies, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-44">6</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/07-chapter-iii-the-native-states/#page-109">71</a>, their amalgamation in 1702, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-44">6</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/07-chapter-iii-the-native-states/#page-109">71</a>; its<br>constitution and administration under<br>the Charter and their development, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-44">6</a>,<br>in England, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-44">6</a>, and in India, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-45">7</a>; its three<br>independent Presidencies, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-45">7</a>; its servants,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-45">7</a>; forced to become a political power<br>from the middle of eighteenth century,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-45">7</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/07-chapter-iii-the-native-states/#page-109">71</a>, by the decay of the Mughal<br>power, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-45">7</a>, by the rise of the Marāthās, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-45">7</a>,<br>by the general unsettlement, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-45">7</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-46">8</a>, and<br>by the aggressions of Dupleix, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-46">8</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/07-chapter-iii-the-native-states/#page-109">71</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/07-chapter-iii-the-native-states/#page-110">72</a>; capture of Pondicherry in 1761,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-46">8</a>; overthrow of French power, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-46">8</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/07-chapter-iii-the-native-states/#page-110">72</a>;<br>political and military ascendancy of the<br>Company in Southern India, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-46">8</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/07-chapter-iii-the-native-states/#page-110">72</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/07-chapter-iii-the-native-states/#page-111">73</a>,<br>and in North India after victory of Plassey (1757), <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-47">9</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/07-chapter-iii-the-native-states/#page-110">72</a>; finally gained in 1772<br>through Clive supreme rights over Bengal, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-47">9</a>; the field register, as a fiscal<br>record, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/11-chapter-vii-land-revenue/#page-247">209</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/11-chapter-vii-land-revenue/#page-248">210</a>, as a record of rights<br>and titles in land, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/11-chapter-vii-land-revenue/#page-249">211</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/11-chapter-vii-land-revenue/#page-250">212</a>; regular<br>and systematic maintenance of cadastral<br>record up to date, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/11-chapter-vii-land-revenue/#page-250">212</a>; its service as a<br>system of registration of title by public<br>entry, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/11-chapter-vii-land-revenue/#page-250">212</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/11-chapter-vii-land-revenue/#page-251">213</a>. assessments are<br>made on net produce or net ‘assets,’ 217;<br>calculation of net produce in different<br>Provinces, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/11-chapter-vii-land-revenue/#page-256">218</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/11-chapter-vii-land-revenue/#page-257">219</a>; (ii) Municipalities of British India, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-322">284</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-336">298</a>:<br>their early origin and development in<br>Presidency towns, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-322">284</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-323">285</a>; their constitution,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-326">288</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-327">289</a>; present municipalities of Calcutta, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-333">295</a>, of Bombay,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-334">296</a>, of Madras, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-334">296</a>, of Rangoon,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-336">298</a>; statistics and schemes for improvement of Calcutta and Bombay,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-335">297</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-336">298</a>. (iii) Local boards, 298–<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-343">305</a>: their late and slow growth, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-336">298</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-337">299</a>; their organization and constitution, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-338">300</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-339">301</a>; popular representation,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-339">301</a>; their functions, receipts, and expenditure, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-339">301</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-342">304</a>; village unions,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-342">304</a>; Port Trusts, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-342">304</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-343">305</a>. Bibliography, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-343">305</a>. Tables of Statistics:<br>I. Of District Municipalities, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-344">306</a>. II.<br>Of Local Boards, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-344">306</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Local boards in rural areas, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-336">298</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-343">305</a>:<br>their origin, a slow and late growth,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-336">298</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-337">299</a>; part of Lord Mayo’s scheme<br>(1871) of financial decentralization, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-337">299</a>,<br>and of Lord Ripon’s (1881) encouragement of representative bodies and local<br>self-government, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-337">299</a>; Acts of 1883–5,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-337">299</a>; their organization and constitution in the different Provinces, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-338">300</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-339">301</a>; various degrees of popular representation, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-339">301</a>; election or nomination<br>of chairman, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-339">301</a>; their functions, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-339">301</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-340">302</a>; their receipts, narrow and mainly<br>from land cesses, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/12-chapter-viii-miscellaneous-revenue/#page-309">271</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/12-chapter-viii-miscellaneous-revenue/#page-310">272</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-340">302</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-341">303</a>;<br>their expenditure, mainly upon roads<br>(not main), <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/12-chapter-viii-miscellaneous-revenue/#page-309">271</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-341">303</a>; upon sanitary<br>and veterinary objects, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-341">303</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-342">304</a>; village<br>unions of Madras, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-338">300</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-342">304</a>. Statistics,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-344">306</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Local Governments: <em>see</em> Provincial Governments.</p><p class="index-entry">Local self-government and representation: by municipalities, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-323">285</a>; under<br>Lord Mayo, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-325">287</a>; their encouragement<br>by Lord Ripon, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-325">287</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-327">289</a>; their present<br>recognition, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-333">295</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-335">297</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-344">306</a>; by local<br>boards, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-337">299</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-339">301</a>; in Port Trusts, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-342">304</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-343">305</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Low-caste children, education of, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/17-chapter-xiii-education/#page-470">432</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Lunacy, causes of its comparative rarity,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/18-chapter-xiv-medical-administration-hospitals-and-sanitation/#page-504">466</a>; its treatment by friends and in<br>Government asylums, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/18-chapter-xiv-medical-administration-hospitals-and-sanitation/#page-504">466</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Lytton, Lord, his financial famine policy,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/10-chapter-vi-finance/#page-203">165</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/10-chapter-vi-finance/#page-205">167</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/10-chapter-vi-finance/#page-226">188</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/10-chapter-vi-finance/#page-227">189</a>; his encouragement<br>of European and Eurasian education,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/17-chapter-xiii-education/#page-472">434</a>.</p><h3 class="sec-h2">M.</h3><p class="index-entry">Macaulay, Lord, drafted the Indian Penal<br>Code, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/09-chapter-v-legislation-and-justice/#page-176">138</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/09-chapter-v-legislation-and-justice/#page-177">139</a>; its excellence, final<br>adoption (1860), and permanence, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/09-chapter-v-legislation-and-justice/#page-176">138</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/09-chapter-v-legislation-and-justice/#page-177">139</a>; instigated first great measure of<br>prison reform, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/16-chapter-xii-police-and-jails/#page-436">398</a>; his minute in<br>favour of Western (not Oriental) education, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/17-chapter-xiii-education/#page-449">411</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Mackenzie, Col. Colin, began topographical survey, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/19-chapter-xv-surveys/#page-528">490</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Madras, Presidency of, large additions to,<br>under Lord Cornwallis, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-48">10</a>; under<br>Lord Wellesley, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-49">11</a>; declared subordinate to Presidency of Bengal, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-52">14</a>;<br>civil administration of by a Governor<br>and Council, appointed by Crown, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-68">30</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-69">31</a>; the powers, privileges, and salary<br>of Governor, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/05-chapter-i-the-government-of-india/#page-69">31</a>; his relations with<br>Council and their conduct of business<br>resemble those of the Governor-General and his Council, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/06-chapter-ii-administrative-divisions/#page-85">47</a>; Native States<br>of, small and unimportant (except Travancore), <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/07-chapter-iii-the-native-states/#page-105">67</a>, their statistics, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/07-chapter-iii-the-native-states/#page-134">96</a>; local<br>Legislative Council, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/09-chapter-v-legislation-and-justice/#page-173">135</a>; its High<br>Court, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/09-chapter-v-legislation-and-justice/#page-185">147</a>; its Presidency Bank, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/20-chapter-xvi-currency-and-banking/#page-562">524</a>;<br>its Survey department, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/19-chapter-xv-surveys/#page-541">503</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/19-chapter-xv-surveys/#page-542">504</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Madras City, municipality of, history of,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-322">284</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-328">290</a>; its present constitution, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/13-chapter-ix-local-and-municipal-government/#page-334">296</a>;<br>its sanitation, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/18-chapter-xiv-medical-administration-hospitals-and-sanitation/#page-511">473</a>. <em>See also</em> Municipalities.</p><p class="index-entry">Madras (Native) States, few and unimportant (except Travancore), <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-04-administrative/chapters/07-chapter-iii-the-native-states/#page-105">67</a>;
